This level is determined through factors such as game history, peer reviews, and self-assessment, all of which contribute to creating fair matches and improving the overall gaming experience. The level is under constant development by Playtomic.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Scenarios for Changing Playtomic Levels</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Users can request a change in their Playtomic level in the following scenarios:</div><ol start="1" class="notion-list notion-list-numbered notion-block" style="list-style-type:decimal"><li><b>Initial Level Adjustment:</b></li><ol class="notion-list notion-list-numbered notion-block" style="list-style-type:lower-alpha"><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>When a new user sets their initial level with the initial questionnaire, they can adjust it by 0.5 points, either up or down.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>The initial level cannot be lower than 0.5 or higher than 5.7.</li></ul></ol></ol><ol start="2" class="notion-list notion-list-numbered notion-block" style="list-style-type:decimal"><li><b>Self-Downgrading:</b></li><ol class="notion-list notion-list-numbered notion-block" style="list-style-type:lower-alpha"><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>Users can self-downgrade their level if they believe it&#x27;s too high.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>This option is available without specific restrictions, and there is currently no cooling-off period between downgrades.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>Users cannot cancel their own self-downgrade or ask Playtomic to do it for them.</li></ul></ol></ol><ol start="3" class="notion-list notion-list-numbered notion-block" style="list-style-type:decimal"><li><b>Peer Review-Based Update:</b></li><ol class="notion-list notion-list-numbered notion-block" style="list-style-type:lower-alpha"><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>Users may be asked to update their level based on peer reviews received after matches.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>This feature ensures that the user&#x27;s level accurately reflects their skill set.</li></ul></ol></ol><ol start="4" class="notion-list notion-list-numbered notion-block" style="list-style-type:decimal"><li><b>Coach-Certified Adjustments:</b></li><ol class="notion-list notion-list-numbered notion-block" style="list-style-type:lower-alpha"><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>A certified coach can adjust a user&#x27;s level based on their assessment during a leveling session.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>This adjustment can increase or decrease the user&#x27;s level and cannot be disputed by the user.</li></ul></ol></ol><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Level Change Policy</b></div><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>Playtomic will not change a user&#x27;s level outside the aforementioned scenarios.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>Requests for level changes outside these methods will not be considered.</li></ul><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Level Adjustments After Matches</b></div><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>After each reservation or competitive match, users&#x27; levels increase or decrease based on the confirmed result:</li><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>A win will increase the winner&#x27;s level.</li><li>A loss will decrease the loser&#x27;s level.</li><li>Draws may also result in level changes depending on the users&#x27; levels involved.</li></ul></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>The amount of change is based on an algorithm that considers the users&#x27; level , users’ reliability and the final result.</li></ul><div class="notion-text notion-block">These mechanisms are designed to ensure that users&#x27; levels accurately reflect their abilities, thus improving the overall gaming experience for everyone involved.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Result Modifications</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Users can add match results as soon as the match or reservation ends within 7 days. The opposing team can confirm these results or propose a different result. The initial team will respond similarly until both teams confirm a result or until 24 hours pass without a team editing or confirming. If the opposing team does not confirm or modify the results within 24 hours, the results are automatically confirmed.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Validity of Results</b></div><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>To be valid, results must be added using the sport-specific result system practiced in the reservation or match.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>An invalid result is considered as a null result.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>Results are only considered valid if all four players who participated in the match are included. This is important because, at times, last-minute substitutions occur, and results are added without updating the players involved.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>Any changes to players must be managed by the club itself and cannot be made once the match has ended.</li></ul><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Editing Confirmed Results</b></div><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>Once results are confirmed, users cannot edit them in the application.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>To request a change, users must contact the customer care team through the appropriate channels.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>The customer service team will confirm in the match chat that both teams agree to the change before proceeding.</li></ul><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Handling Unconfirmed Results</b></div><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>Results that are not confirmed by the opposing team cannot be confirmed by Playtomic, except after 24 hours of the previous result addition.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>If the opposing team tries to confirm an incorrect result, users can only reject it and send the correct one.</li></ul><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Disputes of Results</b></div><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>In cases where players cannot reach an agreement on the results after following the established confirmation process, Playtomic reserves the right to nullify the results.</li></ul><div class="notion-text notion-block">These procedures ensure that match results are accurately recorded and agreed upon by both teams, maintaining fairness and integrity in the game.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>User Misconduct and Community Guidelines</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Playtomic is not responsible for any misconduct by other users that occurs before, during, or after a reservation or match made using our system. Playtomic strives to provide the best possible support through our customer service channels, there are limitations to what we can do:</div><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li><b>Receiving Complaints:</b> We encourage users to report any incidents of misbehavior. These reports help us maintain a record of issues and can be used to address recurring problems.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li><b>Offering Guidance:</b> Our team can provide guidance on how to handle specific situations and recommend appropriate actions or contacts to resolve issues.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li><b>Facilitating Communication:</b> We can help facilitate communication between users and club management to address and resolve disputes or concerns.</li></ul><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Consequences of Misconduct:</b> Users who engage in misconduct may face consequences, including temporary or permanent bans from the Playtomic platform. The severity of the consequence will depend on the nature and frequency of the misconduct.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Privacy and Confidentiality:</b> Playtomic assures users that any reports of misconduct will be handled with discretion and in accordance with applicable privacy laws. We respect the privacy of all parties involved while addressing these issues.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Dispute Resolution Process:</b> In cases of misconduct, we recommend that players first contact the club where the incident occurred, explaining the situation in detail. Playtomic will need to contact the club to fully understand the case. Once we have determined that a user&#x27;s behavior does not respect our community guidelines, that user may be banned from Playtomic.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Club Responsibilities:</b> Clubs are responsible for reporting cases of misconduct to Playtomic and assisting us in determining the responsibilities of all parties involved.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Playtomic will make every effort to take the best decisions possible to ensure the community remains strong. However, in cases of serious misconduct, we recommend users present these cases to local authorities. Playtomic will provide support in the best manner possible to reduce the impact on each user.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Court Conditions</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Playtomic is not responsible for issues related to the condition of the court where a reservation or match is played. This includes matters such as maintenance, quality, or safety concerns of the facility.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">While we aim to support our users through customer service channels, please note the following:</div><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li><b>Reporting Issues:</b> Users should address any problems related to court conditions directly with the club or facility management.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li><b>Support Limitations:</b> Playtomic has no direct authority over court maintenance or safety. Resolution of issues related to court conditions must be handled by club or facility management.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li><b>Accuracy of Information:</b> Only the club is responsible for the information provided on Playtomic. If a user finds the information to be incorrect, they must complain and request a refund directly from the club, not from Playtomic, as Playtomic is not responsible for this information. This information includes, but is not limited to: photographs, logos, court specifications and characteristics, contact information, location, listed amenities, operating hours, and any other descriptive information provided by the club.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li><b>Booking Verification:</b> Users are encouraged to verify booking details directly with the club before arriving, especially for important matches or events.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li><b>Weather Conditions:</b> Playtomic is not responsible for weather-related cancellations or court conditions. Playtomic may assist in facilitating communication but is not responsible for the final resolution.</li></ul><div class="notion-text notion-block">These guidelines are in place to ensure clear understanding of responsibilities among Playtomic, the clubs, and the users. They aim to minimize misunderstandings and provide a framework for addressing potential issues that may arise during the use of our service.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Public Matches No Show</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">The User is aware that, in case of not showing up for a public match, the procedure carried out by Playtomic will be as follows:</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">The “No-Show” procedure may be applied where one or more of the following circumstances occur:</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">A User arrives <b>ten (10) minutes or more</b> after the scheduled start time of the Public Match, or leaves <b>ten (10) minutes or more</b> before the end of the Public Match.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">A User reports an injury <b>before</b> the start of the Public Match.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">A User leaves the Public Match before <b>ninety (90) minutes</b> of play have elapsed.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">A User is replaced. In such cases, any resulting debt and/or charge associated with the “No-Show” procedure shall be applied to the <b>replaced User</b>.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Non-qualifying circumstances (No-Show procedure shall not apply)</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">The “No-Show” procedure shall not be applied in any of the following cases:</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Where the Public Match has a <b>final result recorded</b>, in which case the “No-Show” procedure cannot be applied.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Where an injury occurs <b>during</b> the Public Match.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Where the incident is caused by <b>court and/or facility issues</b> (including, without limitation, poor court conditions, broken lights, or similar circumstances). Such circumstances shall be treated as external factors and Users shall not be penalized under the “No-Show” procedure.</div><ol start="1" class="notion-list notion-list-numbered notion-block" style="list-style-type:decimal"><li><b>Notification:</b> In case a user does not show up for a public match, the remaining users have the option to notify this absence to the club or Playtomic&#x27;s Customer Care via chat or using our email address (hola@playtomic.io).</li></ol><ol start="2" class="notion-list notion-list-numbered notion-block" style="list-style-type:decimal"><li><b>Verification:</b> The customer care team will proceed to confirm the absence by reviewing the chat of the corresponding match or contacting directly with club where the match was played.</li></ol><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>3. Refund and Charge</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Once the absence is confirmed, Playtomic shall manage the corresponding refund for the users who attended the match and shall proceed to charge the user or the users who did not show up.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>3.1 Wallet Payments</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Payments made, in whole or in part, through a club wallet balance are non-refundable. No refunds will be authorized or executed for amounts paid using this payment method.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">If any player arrives late to a match, the group has two options:</div><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li><b>Option A: Wait and Play. </b>If the group decides to wait and play with the late player:</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>The match will be considered fully played</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>No refunds will be issued</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>The full booking fee applies</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>Adding a match result confirms the match was played</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li><b>Option B: Declare No-Show. </b>If the group does not wish to wait:</li><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>They can declare a no-show</li><li>Standard no-show policies and refund procedures will apply</li><li>The late player will receive a no-show penalty</li><li>If a match result is added, the no-show will not be applied and full payment applies</li></ul></ul><div class="notion-text notion-block">This process ensures that present users are properly compensated and that the responsibility derived from the absence is adequately managed.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>5. Resolution:</b> Any disputes regarding Club Wallet balances or transactions must be addressed directly with the relevant club.</li></ul><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>8. DEBT MANAGEMENT</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">A debt is considered when Playtomic does not receive payment from users for a reservation or match, but nevertheless makes the payment to the club on behalf of the users.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Definition of Debt</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">A debt originates when Playtomic covers the payment of a reservation or match because the user(s) involved have not paid their corresponding part. This ensures that the club receives the total amount of the reservation, and Playtomic assumes the responsibility of collecting the amount owed from the users.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Debt Situations</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">A debt is generated in the following circumstances, particularly in cases of split payments, when one or more users do not pay their part within the specified deadline, usually two hours after the end of the match. The process works as follows:</div><ol start="1" class="notion-list notion-list-numbered notion-block" style="list-style-type:decimal"><li>Two hours after the end of the match, Playtomic attempts to charge the unpaid time slots.</li></ol><ol start="2" class="notion-list notion-list-numbered notion-block" style="list-style-type:decimal"><li>If this collection attempt fails, a debt is created in the system.</li></ol><ol start="3" class="notion-list notion-list-numbered notion-block" style="list-style-type:decimal"><li>The user responsible for the unpaid time slots is notified and is blocked from making new reservations until the debt is settled.</li></ol><div class="notion-text notion-block">This mechanism ensures that clubs receive their payments in a timely manner, while Playtomic handles the collection of pending amounts from users. Users are informed about this policy during the reservation process and Playtomic is not responsible for refund requests related to any debt.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Debt Generation by Error</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Occasionally, a debt may be erroneously generated if the club receives payment on-site but does not register it on the club platform. This error results in the allocation of a debt to the owner of the match or reservation, even when payment has been made. In such cases, Playtomic does not assume responsibility for refunding the user. Instead, the club should refund the payment received on-site.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Disputed Payments and Card on File Charges </b>If a User disputes a payment with their bank or payment provider for a charge made through Playtomic, including payments processed through a saved payment method (“Card on File”), Playtomic reserves the right to investigate the case and take appropriate action.When a payment dispute (chargeback) is initiated, the User acknowledges that:The disputed amount may be recorded as an <b>outstanding debt in the User’s Playtomic account</b>.</div><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>Playtomic may <b>temporarily restrict access to certain features or services</b>, including the ability to make new reservations or participate in activities on the platform, until the outstanding balance is resolved.</li></ul><ul class="notion-list notion-list-disc notion-block"><li>Playtomic may contact the User to provide clarification regarding the disputed charge and offer assistance in resolving the matter.</li></ul><div class="notion-text notion-block">Users are encouraged to <b>contact Playtomic Customer Care before initiating a dispute with their bank</b>, so that any potential misunderstanding regarding charges or reservations can be addressed promptly.This procedure applies <b>only to disputes formally initiated with a financial institution or payment provider</b>. Failed payments due to technical issues, expired cards, or insufficient funds do not automatically create a debt with Playtomic and may be handled directly by the corresponding club.Playtomic reserves the right to take reasonable measures to recover outstanding balances resulting from disputed transactions in accordance with applicable laws.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>9. CANCELLATIONS</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">In cases where the User reserves a service using our website or our app, they are assisted by the following rights:</div><div class="notion-text notion-block"><b>Right of Cancellation</b></div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Users have the right to cancel their reservations, subject to the cancellation policy of the corresponding club. To exercise this right of cancellation, the User must cancel directly through our website or app, or by contacting the club directly (when applicable).</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">The cancellation can be entirely done using our app or website, and no additional communication needs to be sent to Playtomic.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">In case of cancellation, Playtomic will process the refund. The refund period will depend on the user&#x27;s bank conditions and can vary. In some cases, Playtomic may apply a cancellation fee to cover the cancellation costs.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">It&#x27;s important to note that once a user has used the service purchased on our website or app, they cannot request any cancellation.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Each club defines its own cancellation policy, which can be reviewed during the reservation process or once it has been made. By making a reservation, the user accepts the cancellation policy of the corresponding club and acknowledges that they cannot cancel the reservation once the period stipulated by said policy has ended.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">If a reservation is made by mistake, during the cancellation policy period, the user cannot edit or cancel it under any circumstance.</div><div class="notion-text notion-block">Clubs can cancel unilaterally a reservation without prior notification to the user.
